  = 0,    // random delay between test stimuli\n36\t    parameter ENTRIES           = 1024, // number of test vector entries\n37\t    parameter LOG2_ENTRIES      = 10    // log base 2 of the number of [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]      bits,\n46\t    output                      val,\n47\t    output reg                  done\n48\t);\n49\t\n50\t    // This is really a parameter, but VCS doesn't like\n51\t    // parameters to be used in left hand sides of always @ *\n52\t    // blocks like I want to use it for the test benches.\n53\t    // Thus, it is a reg.\n54\t    reg [63:0] RANDOM_DELAY; \n55\t\n56\t    //\n57\t    // Internal Parmeters\n58\t    //\n59\t    parameter WAIT_RANDOM    = 1'b0;\n60\t    parameter PUSH_SEND_DATA = 1'b1;\n61\t\n62\t    //\n63\t    // Signal Declarations\n64\t    //\n65\t\n66\t    // Memory to hold test vectors\n67\t    reg [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]     m_f[ENTRIES-1:0];\n68\t\n69\t    // Input index signals\n70\t    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_f;\n71\t    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_next;\n72\t    reg                     index_en;\n73\t\n74\t    // Random delay signals\n75\t    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_f;\n76\t    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_next;\n77\t    reg                     rand_delay_en;\n78\t    reg                     rand_delay_init;\n79\t    \n80\t    // Output queue signals\n81\t    reg [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]    outq_enq_bits;\n82\t    reg                    outq_wr_en;\n83\t    wire                   outq_full;\n84\t\n85\t    // State register\n86\t    reg                    state_f;\n87\t    reg                    state_next;\n88\t\n89\t    //\n90\t    // Synchronous logic\n91\t    //\n92\t\n93\t    // All flip-flops\n94\t    always @ (posedge clk)\n95\t    begin\n96\t        if (~rst_n)\n97\t        begin\n98\t            index_f         <= 0;\n99\t            rand_delay_f    <= 0;\n100\t            state_f         <= WAIT_RANDOM;\n101\t        end\n102\t        else\n103\t        begin\n104\t            index_f         <= index_next;\n105\t            rand_delay_f    <= rand_delay_next;\n106\t            state_f         <= state_next;\n107\t        end\n108\t    end\n109\t\n110\t    //\n111\t    // Combinational logic\n112\t    //\n113\t\n114\t    // Entry index next value\n115\t    always @ *\n116\t    begin\n117\t        index_next = index_f;\n118\t        if (index_en)\n119\t            index_next = index_f + 1;\n120\t    end\n121\t\n122\t    // Random delay counter next value\n123\t    always @ *\n124\t    begin\n125\t        rand_delay_next = rand_delay_f;\n126\t        if (rand_delay_en)\n127\t            rand_delay_next = rand_delay_f - 1;\n128\t        else if (rand_delay_init)\n129\t            rand_delay_next = {$random} % RANDOM_DELAY;\n130\t    end\n131\t\n132\t    // Next state logic\n133\t    always @ *\n134\t    begin\n135\t        state_next = state_f;\n136\t        case (state_f)\n137\t            WAIT_RANDOM:\n138\t            begin\n139\t                if (!outq_full && !done && (rand_delay_f == 0))\n140\t                    state_next = PUSH_SEND_DATA;\n141\t            end\n142\t            PUSH_SEND_DATA:\n143\t            begin\n144\t                if (outq_full || done || (RANDOM_DELAY > 0))\n145\t                begin\n146\t                    state_next = WAIT_RANDOM;\n147\t                end\n148\t            end\n149\t        endcase\n150\t    end\n151\t\n152\t    // Enable bits\n153\t    always @ *\n154\t    begin\n155\t        index_en        = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & ~outq_full & ~done;\n156\t        rand_delay_en   = (state_f == WAIT_RANDOM) & (rand_delay_f > 0) & ~done; \n157\t        rand_delay_init = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & (RANDOM_DELAY > 0) & ~done;\n158\t    end\n159\t\n160\t    // Output queue signals\n161\t    always @ *\n162\t    begin\n163\t        outq_wr_en      = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & ~outq_full && ~done;\n164\t        outq_enq_bits   = m_f[index_f];\n165\t output reg                  done\n);\n\n    // This is really a parameter, but VCS doesn't like\n    // parameters to be used in left hand sides of always @ *\n    // blocks like I want to use it for the test benches.\n    // Thus, it is a reg.\n    reg [63:0] RANDOM_DELAY; \n\n    //\n    // Internal Parmeters\n    //\n    parameter WAIT_RANDOM    = 1'b0;\n    parameter PUSH_SEND_DATA = 1'b1;\n\n    //\n    // Signal Declarations\n    //\n\n    // Memory to hold test vectors\n    reg [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]     m_f[ENTRIES-1:0];\n\n    // Input index signals\n    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_f;\n    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_next;\n    reg                     index_en;\n\n    // Random delay signals\n    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_f;\n    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_next;\n outq_wr_en;\n    wire                   outq_full;\n\n    // State register\n    reg                    state_f;\n    reg                    state_next;\n\n    //\n    // Synchronous logic\n    //\n\n    // All flip-flops\n    always @ (posedge clk)\n    begin\n        if (~rst_n)\n        begin\n            index_f         <= 0;\n            rand_delay_f    <= 0;\n            state_f         <= WAIT_RANDOM;\n        end\n        else\n        begin\n            index_f         <= index_next;\n            rand_delay_f    <= rand_delay_next;\n            state_f         <= state_next;\n        end\n    end\n\n    //\n    // Combinational logic\n    //\n\n    // Entry index next value\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        index_next = index_f;\n        if (index_en)\n            index_next = rand_delay_f - 1;\n        else if (rand_delay_init)\n            rand_delay_next = {$random} % RANDOM_DELAY;\n    end\n\n    // Next state logic\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        state_next = state_f;\n        case (state_f)\n            WAIT_RANDOM:\n            begin\n                if (!outq_full && !done && (rand_delay_f == 0))\n                    state_next = PUSH_SEND_DATA;\n            end\n            PUSH_SEND_DATA:\n            begin\n                if (outq_full || done || (RANDOM_DELAY > 0))\n                begin\n                    state_next = WAIT_RANDOM;\n   = (state_f == WAIT_RANDOM) & (rand_delay_f > 0) & ~done; \n        rand_delay_init = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & (RANDOM_DELAY > 0) & ~done;\n    end\n\n    // Output queue signals\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        outq_wr_en      = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & ~outq_full && ~done;\n        outq_enq_bits   = m_f[index_f];\n    end\n\n    // Done signal\n    always @ *\n        done = ((m_f[index_f] === {BIT_WIDTH{1'bx}}) | (index_f == (ENTRIES-1)));\n\n    //\n    // Helper module instantiations\n    //\n\n    // Output queue\n    test_infrstrct_fifo\n    #(\n        .BIT_WIDTH When you do string assignment in Verilog, strings are right-justified and zero-padded on the left. So `test_cases_path = \"path/\"` sets the LAST N bytes to the string characters, and the rest are zeros.\n\nWhen concatenated: `{test_cases_path, test_case, \"_src.vmh\"}` - this creates a giant string where test_cases_path is the leftmost 1024 bytes (mostly zeros/spaces), test_case is the next 64 bytes, then \"_src.vmh\".\n\nBut when passed to `$readmemb`, iverilog treats this as a path string and might be including the null bytes as part of the filename. This is likely an iverilog 11 quirk where string handling in concatenations doesn't strip the padding like it should. bit vectors means they get concatenated with all their bit width included, creating a giant padded string.\n\nBut this behavior should be the same in iverilog 11 and 12.
